	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }

	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "何を言っているのかはわからない。",
		   NULL, "Ne hakkında konuştuğunu bilmiyorum.", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }

	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "……口調からして…独り言？",
		   NULL, "...Ses tonuna bakılırsa... kendi kendine mi konuşuyordu?", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }

	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "それとも…そこにいる誰かに言っているのか？",
		   NULL, "Yoksa... oradaki birine mi sesleniyordu?", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }

	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "その何かを聞き取ろうと…神経を研ぎ澄ます…。",
		   NULL, "Biraz daha fazlasını duyabilmek umuduyla... tüm duyularımı keskinleştirdim...", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }

	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "何を伝えようとしたんだ、彼は……。",
		   NULL, "Neyi... anlatmaya çalışıyordu...?", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }

	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "一体……何を………！！！",
		   NULL, "Bu da... neyin nesi olabilirdi...?!?!", Line_Normal);
	ClearMessage();

	DrawScene( "black", 200 );

	OutputLine(NULL, "　ガチャン！",
		   NULL, " *klik*", Line_WaitForInput);
	OutputLine(NULL, "　ツーツーツーツーツー…。",
		   NULL, " *düt* *düt* *düt* *düt* ...", Line_Normal);
	ClearMessage();

	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "あまりに唐突に切れた。……１０円が切れたのか？！",
		   NULL, "Hat aniden kesildi. ...10 yenlik madeni paranın süresi mi bitti acaba?!", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }

	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "公衆電話だったから…！！",
		   NULL, "Sonuçta ankesörlü telefondu...!!", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }

	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLine("<color=#a59da9>大石</color>", NULL, "<color=#a59da9>Ooishi</color>", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	ModPlayVoiceLS(4, 11, "ps3/s27/11/nois_0430", 128, TRUE);
	OutputLine(NULL, "「…………あ」",
		   NULL, "\"......Ah.\"", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }

	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "唐突に切られたから。",
		   NULL, "Ve telefon böyle aniden kapandığı için,", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }

	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "…むしろ最後の一言が鮮明に脳裏に戻った。",
		   NULL, "...son sözleri zihnimde tüm canlılığıyla yankılanmaya devam etti.", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }

	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLine("<color=#a59da9>大石</color>", NULL, "<color=#a59da9>Ooishi</color>", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	ModPlayVoiceLS(4, 11, "ps3/s27/11/nois_0440", 128, TRUE);
	OutputLine(NULL, "「……にぱ～☆、だ」",
		   NULL, "\"'...Nipah~☆' dedi.\"", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }

	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "その時、直感した。",
		   NULL, "Tam o anda içime bir his doğdu.", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }

	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "…………もう、そんなに急ぐことがないことに。",
		   NULL, "......Artık acele etmeye hiç gerek olmadığı hissi.", Line_Normal);
	ClearMessage();

	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "　こうして１人の不幸な少年が、またしても雛見沢の闇に飲み込まれたのであった…。",
		   NULL, "İşte talihsiz bir genç çocuk, Hinamizawa'nın karanlığı tarafından bir kez daha böyle yutuldu...", Line_Normal);
	ClearMessage();

	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode) == 0) { ClearMessage(); }
	DisableWindow();
	PlaySE( 0, "a6", 128, 64 );
	DrawScene( "white", 3000 );
	DrawScene( "effect/end_2", 2000 );
	DrawScene( "effect/end_3", 4000 );

	Wait( 1000 );
	DrawScene( "black", 1000 );

	//;■完

	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode) == 0) { ClearMessage(); }

	GetAchievement("HIGURASHI_STORY_EP09_BONUS_02");
}
